```
1	"""Build a deployable SD-card image from a mother image — the build app.
2	
3	`build` is one of the three image-builder apps (`build`, `capture`,
4	`write`) and the fully *rootless* one (sanitize + inject + finalize): it
5	imports only `imglib` (the rootless storage toolkit) and `imgcfg`
6	(shared config), never the device layer — so "build never touches a
7	physical device" holds structurally, not just by convention.
8	
9	Given a mother image (produced by `capture`, or any existing `.img`),
10	`build` copies it to the output and edits the output's ext4 root in the
11	file with `debugfs` — no loop-mount, no root:
12	
13	- transform: copy mother -> output (the mother stays pristine),
14	- sanitize: scrub the output's secrets (logs, ssh host keys, machine-id,
15	  shell history, wifi creds),
16	- inject: write the per-deployment `fc-config.toml` + an agetty-autologin
17	  autostart that runs fc on the console at boot,
18	- finalize: shrink the rootfs to its minimum, then xz the image.
19	
20	Settings come from `fc-config.toml`'s `[image]` (shared artifact paths)
21	and `[build]` (this app's knobs); see `imgcfg` for the config / env / CLI
22	precedence. `--dry-run` resolves and prints the plan, touching nothing.
23	
24	    # show the resolved plan, change nothing
25	    uv run python scripts/build.py --dry-run
26	
27	    # build a deployable image from a mother (rootless)
28	    uv run python scripts/build.py \\
29	        --mother images/mother.img --output images/fc-deploy.img \\
30	        --no-shrink --no-compress
31	"""
32	
33	import argparse
34	import os
35	import shutil
36	import tempfile
37	from pathlib import Path
38	
39	import imgcfg
40	import imglib
41	
42	# fc-config.toml tables this app reads: shared artifact paths + the
43	# build-specific knobs.
44	SECTIONS = ("image", "build")
45	
46	# Built-in fallback used only when fc-config.toml omits a key, so the
47	# tool still resolves a complete plan against a stripped config. These
48	# mirror the committed [image] + [build] defaults.
49	DEFAULTS: dict[str, object] = {
50	    "images_dir": "images",
51	    "mother": "mother.img",
52	    "output": "fc-deploy.img",
53	    "fc_path": "/home/alpine/data/prgs/nps-gnc/fc",
54	    "fc_config_target": "",
55	    "autologin_tty": "tty1",
56	    "scrub_logs": True,
57	    "scrub_ssh_host_keys": True,
58	    "scrub_machine_id": True,
59	    "scrub_shell_history": True,
60	    "scrub_wifi": True,
61	    "shrink": True,
62	    "compress": True,
63	}
64	
65	# Path-valued settings (free-form strings) vs. on/off toggles. The split
66	# drives both the argparse wiring and the FC_* env coercion.
67	STR_KEYS = ("images_dir", "mother", "output", "fc_path",
68	            "fc_config_target", "autologin_tty")
69	BOOL_KEYS = (
70	    "scrub_logs",
71	    "scrub_ssh_host_keys",
72	    "scrub_machine_id",
73	    "scrub_shell_history",
74	    "scrub_wifi",
75	    "shrink",
76	    "compress",
77	)
78	
79	
80	def config_target(plan: dict) -> str:
81	    """In-image path for the injected fc-config.toml (under fc_path by default)."""
82	    return plan["fc_config_target"][0] or f"{plan['fc_path'][0]}/fc-config.toml"
83	
84	
85	def print_plan(config_path: Path, plan: dict) -> None:
86	    """Print the resolved settings and the stages the build would run.
87	
88	    - the settings block (shared formatting) shows each value and where
89	      it won (config / env / flag),
90	    - the stages block reads the plan into the ordered actions a real
91	      build performs.
92	    """
93	    imgcfg.print_settings(config_path, plan, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS, "build")
94	
95	    def val(key: str):
96	        return plan[key][0]
97	
98	    scrubs = [k.removeprefix("scrub_") for k in BOOL_KEYS
99	              if k.startswith("scrub_") and val(k)]
100	
101	    mother, output = imgcfg.img_path(plan, "mother"), imgcfg.img_path(plan, "output")
102	    print("")
103	    print("stages:")
104	    print(f"  1. transform copy {mother} -> {output}")
105	    print(f"  2. sanitize  scrub: {', '.join(scrubs) if scrubs else '(none)'}")
106	    print(f"  3. inject    {config_target(plan)} + agetty autologin on "
107	          f"{val('autologin_tty')} (run fc at boot)")
108	    finalize_steps = []
109	    if val("shrink"):
110	        finalize_steps.append("shrink rootfs")
111	    if val("compress"):
112	        finalize_steps.append(f"xz -> {output}.xz")
113	    print(f"  4. finalize  {', '.join(finalize_steps) if finalize_steps else '(none)'}")
114	
115	
116	# An empty source file every truncate-in-place write() points at, so a
117	# log / machine-id ends up present but 0 bytes with its mode restored.
118	_EMPTY = Path("/dev/null")
119	
120	
121	def _scrub_ssh_host_keys(root_fs: Path) -> list[str]:
122	    """debugfs commands deleting /etc/ssh/ssh_host_* (regenerated on boot)."""
123	    cmds = [f"rm {e['path']}" for e in imglib.fs_list(root_fs, "/etc/ssh")
124	            if e["name"].startswith("ssh_host_") and e["mode"] & 0o170000 == 0o100000]
125	    print(f"  scrub_ssh_host_keys: {len(cmds)} key file(s)")
126	    return cmds
127	
128	
129	def _scrub_wifi(root_fs: Path) -> list[str]:
130	    """debugfs commands deleting wpa_supplicant + NetworkManager creds."""
131	    cmds = []
132	    for e in imglib.fs_list(root_fs, "/etc/wpa_supplicant"):
133	        if e["name"].startswith("wpa_supplicant") and e["name"].endswith(".conf"):
134	            cmds.append(f"rm {e['path']}")
135	    for e in imglib.fs_list(root_fs, "/etc/NetworkManager/system-connections"):
136	        if e["mode"] & 0o170000 == 0o100000:
137	            cmds.append(f"rm {e['path']}")
138	    print(f"  scrub_wifi: {len(cmds)} credential file(s)")
139	    return cmds
140	
141	
142	def _scrub_shell_history(root_fs: Path) -> list[str]:
143	    """debugfs commands deleting shell history in /root and each /home/*."""
144	    names = {".bash_history", ".zsh_history", ".python_history", ".sh_history"}
145	    homes = ["/root"] + [e["path"] for e in imglib.fs_list(root_fs, "/home")
146	                         if e["mode"] & 0o170000 == 0o040000]
147	    cmds = []
148	    for home in homes:
149	        for e in imglib.fs_list(root_fs, home):
150	            if e["name"] in names and e["mode"] & 0o170000 == 0o100000:
151	                cmds.append(f"rm {e['path']}")
152	    print(f"  scrub_shell_history: {len(cmds)} history file(s)")
153	    return cmds
154	
155	
156	def _truncate_cmds(entry: dict) -> list[str]:
157	    """debugfs commands to empty a file in place, restoring its mode/owner.
158	
159	    debugfs has no truncate, so delete the file then write an empty one
160	    back at the same path and re-stamp the original mode / uid / gid.
161	    """
162	    return [
163	        f"rm {entry['path']}",
164	        f"write {_EMPTY} {entry['path']}",
165	        f"sif {entry['path']} mode 0{entry['mode']:o}",
166	        f"sif {entry['path']} uid {entry['uid']}",
167	        f"sif {entry['path']} gid {entry['gid']}",
168	    ]
169	
170	
171	def _scrub_logs(root_fs: Path) -> list[str]:
172	    """debugfs commands truncating every regular file under /var/log.
173	
174	    Services expect their log files to exist, so empty rather than delete.
175	    """
176	    cmds = []
177	    n = 0
178	    for entry in imglib.fs_walk_files(root_fs, "/var/log"):
179	        cmds += _truncate_cmds(entry)
180	        n += 1
181	    print(f"  scrub_logs: {n} file(s) under /var/log")
182	    return cmds
183	
184	
185	def _scrub_machine_id(root_fs: Path) -> list[str]:
186	    """debugfs commands blanking /etc/machine-id and dbus's copy in place."""
187	    cmds = []
188	    n = 0
189	    for path in ("/etc/machine-id", "/var/lib/dbus/machine-id"):
190	        entry = next((e for e in imglib.fs_list(root_fs, path.rsplit("/", 1)[0])
191	                      if e["path"] == path and e["mode"] & 0o170000 == 0o100000), None)
192	        if entry:
193	            cmds += _truncate_cmds(entry)
194	            n += 1
195	    print(f"  scrub_machine_id: {n} machine-id file(s)")
196	    return cmds
197	
198	
199	def sanitize_root(root_fs: Path, plan: dict) -> None:
200	    """Run the enabled scrub passes against an extracted ext4 root file.
201	
202	    - each pass enumerates targets (rootless `debugfs` reads) and returns
203	      its edit commands; all commands run in one batched `debugfs -w`,
204	    - `root_fs` is a standalone partition image (offset 0), so debugfs
205	      addresses it directly.
206	    """
207	    def on(key: str) -> bool:
208	        return plan[key][0]
209	
210	    cmds: list[str] = []
211	    if on("scrub_logs"):
212	        cmds += _scrub_logs(root_fs)
213	    if on("scrub_ssh_host_keys"):
214	        cmds += _scrub_ssh_host_keys(root_fs)
215	    if on("scrub_machine_id"):
216	        cmds += _scrub_machine_id(root_fs)
217	    if on("scrub_shell_history"):
218	        cmds += _scrub_shell_history(root_fs)
219	    if on("scrub_wifi"):
220	        cmds += _scrub_wifi(root_fs)
221	    imglib.debugfs_batch(root_fs, cmds)
222	
223	
224	# Login user the agetty autologin lands on; `fc_path` and the injected
225	# config sit under this user's home by default.
226	_AUTOLOGIN_USER = "alpine"
227	
228	# Marker on the `.profile` launch block so a rebuild appends it once.
229	_PROFILE_MARKER = "# fc autostart (build inject)"
230	
231	
232	def _entry(root_fs: Path, path: str) -> dict | None:
233	    """Return the directory entry (mode/uid/gid/...) for `path`, or None."""
234	    parent, _, name = path.rpartition("/")
235	    for e in imglib.fs_list(root_fs, parent or "/"):
236	        if e["name"] == name:
237	            return e
238	    return None
239	
240	
241	def _stage(text: str, tmps: list[Path]) -> Path:
242	    """Write `text` to a fresh temp file tracked in `tmps`; return its path."""
243	    fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix="fc-inject-")
244	    with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f:
245	        f.write(text)
246	    p = Path(name)
247	    tmps.append(p)
248	    return p
249	
250	
251	def _replace_file_cmds(root_fs: Path, path: str, host_src: Path, entry: dict | None,
252	                       default_mode: int, default_uid: int, default_gid: int) -> list[str]:
253	    """debugfs commands to overwrite `path` from `host_src`, restoring metadata.
254	
255	    - debugfs has no in-place edit, so delete (if present) then write the
256	      new content and re-stamp mode / uid / gid,
257	    - `entry` is the file's prior directory entry (its mode/uid/gid are
258	      preserved); when it's absent the defaults stamp a fresh file.
259	    """
260	    mode = entry["mode"] if entry else default_mode
261	    uid = entry["uid"] if entry else default_uid
262	    gid = entry["gid"] if entry else default_gid
263	    cmds = []
264	    if entry is not None:
265	        cmds.append(f"rm {path}")
266	    cmds.append(f"write {host_src} {path}")
267	    cmds.append(f"sif {path} mode 0{mode:o}")
268	    cmds.append(f"sif {path} uid {uid}")
269	    cmds.append(f"sif {path} gid {gid}")
270	    return cmds
271	
272	
273	def _inittab_autologin(content: str, tty: str) -> str:
274	    """Return inittab text with `tty`'s getty line swapped for agetty autologin.
275	
276	    - the matching line is the non-comment entry whose inittab id field
277	      is `tty`; it becomes an `agetty --autologin <user>` respawn,
278	    - matching the existing line keeps the rewrite idempotent (a second
279	      build replaces the agetty line with itself); if no line matches,
280	      the agetty line is appended.
281	    """
282	    new_line = (f"{tty}::respawn:/sbin/agetty --autologin "
283	                f"{_AUTOLOGIN_USER} {tty} linux")
284	    out, replaced = [], False
285	    for line in content.splitlines():
286	        stripped = line.strip()
287	        if not stripped.startswith("#") and stripped.split(":", 1)[0] == tty:
288	            out.append(new_line)
289	            replaced = True
290	        else:
291	            out.append(line)
292	    if not replaced:
293	        out.append(new_line)
294	    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"
295	
296	
297	def _profile_launch(content: str, fc_path: str, tty: str) -> str:
298	    """Return `.profile` text with a tty-guarded fc launch appended.
299	
300	    - the launch runs fc only on the autologin console (`/dev/<tty>`), so
301	      ssh and other logins drop to a normal shell,
302	    - a marker comment makes the append idempotent across rebuilds, and
303	      prior `.profile` content is preserved ahead of it.
304	    """
305	    if _PROFILE_MARKER in content:
306	        return content
307	    launch = (
308	        f"\n{_PROFILE_MARKER}\n"
309	        f'[ "$(tty)" = /dev/{tty} ] && cd {fc_path} && uv run fc\n'
310	    )
311	    base = content if (not content or content.endswith("\n")) else content + "\n"
312	    return base + launch
313	
314	
315	def inject_root(root_fs: Path, plan: dict, config_src: Path) -> None:
316	    """Inject the per-deployment fc-config.toml + an agetty-autologin autostart.
317	
318	    - writes the build's fc-config.toml to `fc_config_target` (under
319	      `fc_path` by default), overwriting the tree's copy,
320	    - rewrites `/etc/inittab` so `autologin_tty` autologs in as
321	      `alpine`, and appends a tty-guarded `cd <fc_path> && uv run fc` to
322	      that user's `.profile`, so fc runs on the console at boot exactly
323	      like `just run` — and only there, not on ssh logins,
324	    - removes any interim `/etc/local.d/fc.start` a prior build left.
325	
326	    This replaces the interim `local.d` autostart: OpenRC's `local`
327	    service runs boot commands synchronously, so a long-running
328	    foreground fc would freeze boot. `agetty` is an image prereq; the
329	    exact tty / flags are validated on hardware at close-out.
330	    """
331	    fc_path = plan["fc_path"][0]
332	    tty = plan["autologin_tty"][0]
333	    target = config_target(plan)
334	    home = f"/home/{_AUTOLOGIN_USER}"
335	    profile = f"{home}/.profile"
336	
337	    inittab = imglib.fs_read(root_fs, "/etc/inittab")
338	    if inittab is None:
339	        raise SystemExit("build: /etc/inittab not found in image root")
340	    new_inittab = _inittab_autologin(inittab, tty)
341	    new_profile = _profile_launch(imglib.fs_read(root_fs, profile) or "", fc_path, tty)
342	
343	    it_entry = _entry(root_fs, "/etc/inittab")
344	    pr_entry = _entry(root_fs, profile)
345	    home_entry = _entry(root_fs, home)
346	    # A fresh .profile inherits the home dir's owner (the alpine user).
347	    new_uid = home_entry["uid"] if home_entry else 1000
348	    new_gid = home_entry["gid"] if home_entry else 1000
349	
350	    tmps: list[Path] = []
351	    try:
352	        cmds: list[str] = []
353	        # 1. per-deployment config (overwrite the tree's copy)
354	        cfg_entry = _entry(root_fs, target)
355	        cmds += _replace_file_cmds(root_fs, target, config_src, cfg_entry,
356	                                   0o100644, new_uid, new_gid)
357	        # 2. inittab: autologin on <tty>
358	        cmds += _replace_file_cmds(root_fs, "/etc/inittab",
359	                                   _stage(new_inittab, tmps), it_entry,
360	                                   0o100644, 0, 0)
361	        # 3. .profile: tty-guarded fc launch
362	        cmds += _replace_file_cmds(root_fs, profile,
363	                                   _stage(new_profile, tmps), pr_entry,
364	                                   0o100644, new_uid, new_gid)
365	        # 4. drop the interim local.d autostart if a prior build left one
366	        if imglib.fs_exists(root_fs, "/etc/local.d/fc.start"):
367	            cmds.append("rm /etc/local.d/fc.start")
368	        imglib.debugfs_batch(root_fs, cmds)
369	    finally:
370	        for t in tmps:
371	            t.unlink(missing_ok=True)
372	
373	    print(f"  inject: {target} + agetty autologin on {tty} ({profile} launch)")
374	
375	
376	def edit_root(img: Path, plan: dict, config_src: Path) -> None:
377	    """Extract `img`'s ext4 root, sanitize + inject it, splice it back.
378	
379	    - the root partition is located by filesystem probe, copied out to a
380	      standalone temp file so `debugfs` can edit it (scrub then inject),
381	      then written back at the same offset,
382	    - one extract / splice covers both passes; the temp file is always
383	      removed, even on failure.
384	    """
385	    boot, root, _parts = imglib.find_boot_root(img)
386	    if root is None:
387	        raise SystemExit(f"build: no ext root partition found in {img}")
388	    print(f"\nedit: root@{root['off']} ({root['length']} B), "
389	          f"boot={'@' + str(boot['off']) if boot else '(none)'}")
390	
391	    root_fs = imglib.extract_region(img, root["off"], root["length"])
392	    try:
393	        sanitize_root(root_fs, plan)
394	        inject_root(root_fs, plan, config_src)
395	        imglib.splice_region(img, root["off"], root_fs)
396	    finally:
397	        root_fs.unlink(missing_ok=True)
398	
399	
400	def finalize(img: Path, plan: dict) -> None:
401	    """Shrink the root partition to minimum and/or xz-compress the output.
402	
403	    - shrink (rootless): re-extract the ext root, `resize2fs -M` it, splice the
404	      smaller fs back, shrink its partition-table entry, and truncate the image
405	      so the deploy `.img` is no longer the mother's full size,
406	    - compress: `xz` the (shrunk) image to `<output>.xz`, keeping the `.img` so
407	      it stays directly writable,
408	    - the shrunk rootfs sits at its minimum (no free space) by design; growing
409	      it to the target device is the write app's job (0.3.0-5.4).
410	    """
411	    def val(key: str):
412	        return plan[key][0]
413	
414	    if val("shrink"):
415	        _boot, root, parts = imglib.find_boot_root(img)
416	        if root is None:
417	            raise SystemExit(f"build: no ext root partition in {img}")
418	        partnum = parts.index(root) + 1
419	        print(f"\nshrink: root partition #{partnum} @ {root['off']}")
420	        root_fs = imglib.extract_region(img, root["off"], root["length"])
421	        try:
422	            new_size = imglib.ext_min_resize(root_fs)
423	            os.truncate(root_fs, new_size)
424	            imglib.splice_region(img, root["off"], root_fs)
425	        finally:
426	            root_fs.unlink(missing_ok=True)
427	        imglib.set_part_size(img, partnum, new_size // imglib.sector_size(img))
428	        os.truncate(img, root["off"] + new_size)
429	        print(f"  shrink: root -> {new_size} B; image -> {root['off'] + new_size} B")
430	
431	    if val("compress"):
432	        print(f"\ncompress: xz -k {img} -> {img}.xz")
433	        imglib.run(["xz", "-k", "-T0", "-f", str(img)], capture=True)
434	
435	
436	def run_build(plan: dict, config_src: Path) -> int:
437	    """Execute the build stages: transform + sanitize + inject + finalize.
438	
439	    - copies the mother to the output (relative paths sit under
440	      `images_dir`) so the mother stays pristine, then edits the copy's
441	      ext4 root in place, then finalize (shrink + xz),
442	    - capture (device -> mother) and write (output -> device) are separate
443	      apps; build assumes the mother already exists.
444	    """
445	    mother = imgcfg.img_path(plan, "mother")
446	    output = imgcfg.img_path(plan, "output")
447	    if not mother.exists():
448	        raise SystemExit(
449	            f"build: mother image not found: {mother} "
450	            "(run `just capture` or point --mother at an existing .img)")
451	
452	    print(f"\ntransform: copy {mother} -> {output}")
453	    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
454	    shutil.copy2(mother, output)
455	    edit_root(output, plan, config_src)
456	    finalize(output, plan)
457	
458	    print("\nbuild: transform + sanitize + inject + finalize complete.")
459	    return 0
460	
461	
462	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
463	    """Build the parser: --config/--dry-run plus one flag per [image]/[build] key."""
464	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
465	        prog="build",
466	        description=__doc__.splitlines()[0],
467	    )
468	    p.add_argument(
469	        "--config", type=Path, default=imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG,
470	        help=f"fc-config.toml to read [image]/[build] from (default: {imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG})",
471	    )
472	    p.add_argument(
473	        "--dry-run", action="store_true", dest="dry_run",
474	        help="resolve and print the plan, mutate nothing",
475	    )
476	    imgcfg.add_arguments(p, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)
477	    return p.parse_args()
478	
479	
480	def main() -> int:
481	    """Resolve the plan, print it, then run the build unless --dry-run."""
482	    args = parse_args()
483	    file_cfg = imgcfg.load_config(args.config, SECTIONS, DEFAULTS)
484	    plan = imgcfg.resolve(args, file_cfg, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)
485	    print_plan(args.config, plan)
486	
487	    if args.dry_run:
488	        return 0
489	    return run_build(plan, args.config)
490	
491	
492	if __name__ == "__main__":
493	    raise SystemExit(main())
494	
```

 root_fs = imglib.extract_region(output, root[\"off\"], root[\"length\"])\n    try:\n        assert imglib.fs_read(root_fs, \"/etc/app.conf\") == \"key = value\\n\"\n        assert imglib.fs_read(root_fs, \"/opt/app/assets/top.txt\") == \"top\\n\"\n        assert imglib.fs_read(root_fs, \"/opt/app/assets/sub/b.txt\") == \"nested\\n\"\n        assert imglib.fs_read(root_fs, mk_test_image.STALE_PATH) == \"FRESH CONFIG\\n\"\n    finally:\n        root_fs.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n\n\ndef test_dry_run_mutates_nothing(tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):\n    \"\"\"--dry-run resolves and prints the plan but writes no output image.\"\"\"\n    mother = tmp_path / \"mother.img\"\n    output = tmp_path / \"deploy.img\"\n    mk_test_image.build(mother)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, \"argv\", [\n        \"bd-build\", \"--dry-run\",\n        \"--mother\", str(mother), \"--output\", str(output),\n        \"--add\", f\"{tmp_path / 'x'}:/etc/x\",\n    ])\n    assert build.main() == 0\n    assert not output.exists()\n    assert \"stages:\" in capsys.readouterr().out\n"
```json
}
```

```
1	"""Capture a source block device into the mother image — the capture app.
2	
3	`capture` is one of the three image-builder apps (`build`, `capture`,
4	`write`). It reads a source block device — an SD card, USB stick, NVMe,
5	or disk — into `[image].mother` (via the device layer, `devsafe`) so a
6	subsequent `build` can transform it. Reading a block device needs root,
7	so the `dd` runs under `sudo`; the surrounding inspection (device
8	resolution, `--list-devices`) stays rootless.
9	
10	capture is a *non-destructive read*, so it takes a single source device
11	(`--source`, a `/dev/xx` node or a `/dev/disk/by-id/yy` handle) — no
12	two-key targeting and no confirm (that safety guards the destructive
13	write, not this). `--test-mode` reads a regular file instead of a block
14	device, so the capture path is exercisable against a synthetic
15	mk_test_image `.img` with no root or hardware.
16	
17	Settings come from `fc-config.toml`'s `[image]` (shared artifact paths)
18	and `[capture]` (this app's `source`); see `imgcfg` for the config / env
19	/ CLI precedence. `--dry-run` resolves and prints the plan, touching
20	nothing.
21	
22	    # list candidate devices (with by-id), then exit
23	    uv run python scripts/capture.py --list-devices
24	
25	    # capture a device into the mother (needs sudo)
26	    uv run python scripts/capture.py --source /dev/sdb
27	
28	    # exercise the capture path against a synthetic file, no root
29	    uv run python scripts/capture.py --test-mode \\
30	        --source images/_test-card.img --mother images/_test-mother.img
31	"""
32	
33	import argparse
34	from pathlib import Path
35	
36	import devsafe
37	import imgcfg
38	
39	# fc-config.toml tables this app reads: shared artifact paths + capture's
40	# own knobs.
41	SECTIONS = ("image", "capture")
42	
43	# Built-in fallback mirroring the committed [image] + [capture] defaults,
44	# so the tool resolves a complete plan against a stripped config.
45	DEFAULTS: dict[str, object] = {
46	    "images_dir": "images",
47	    "mother": "mother.img",
48	    "source": "",
49	}
50	
51	STR_KEYS = ("images_dir", "mother", "source")
52	BOOL_KEYS = ()
53	
54	
55	def print_plan(config_path: Path, plan: dict) -> None:
56	    """Print the resolved settings and the capture stage."""
57	    imgcfg.print_settings(config_path, plan, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS, "capture")
58	    source = plan["source"][0]
59	    mother = imgcfg.img_path(plan, "mother")
60	    print("")
61	    print("stages:")
62	    if source:
63	        print(f"  1. capture   dd if={source} of={mother}")
64	    else:
65	        print("  1. capture   (no source set — nothing to capture)")
66	
67	
68	def run_capture(plan: dict, *, test_mode: bool) -> int:
69	    """Capture the source device into the mother image.
70	
71	    - resolves the single `--source` handle (`/dev/xx` or by-id) to a
72	      device, then copies it into `[image].mother` via devsafe,
73	    - `test_mode` reads a regular file instead of a block device so the
74	      path is self-testable; otherwise the read runs under `sudo dd`.
75	    """
76	    source = plan["source"][0]
77	    if not source:
78	        raise SystemExit("capture: no --source device given (nothing to capture)")
79	    mother = imgcfg.img_path(plan, "mother")
80	    dev = devsafe.resolve_device(source, test_mode=test_mode)
81	    print(f"\ncapture: {dev} -> {mother}{' (test-mode)' if test_mode else ''}")
82	    devsafe.dd_capture(dev, mother, test_mode=test_mode)
83	    print("\ncapture: card -> mother complete.")
84	    return 0
85	
86	
87	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
88	    """Build the parser: control flags + one flag per [image]/[capture] key."""
89	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
90	        prog="capture",
91	        description=__doc__.splitlines()[0],
92	    )
93	    p.add_argument(
94	        "--config", type=Path, default=imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG,
95	        help=f"fc-config.toml to read [image]/[capture] from (default: {imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG})",
96	    )
97	    p.add_argument(
98	        "--dry-run", action="store_true", dest="dry_run",
99	        help="resolve and print the plan, mutate nothing",
100	    )
101	    p.add_argument(
102	        "--list-devices", action="store_true", dest="list_devices",
103	        help="list block devices (with by-id) for picking a source, then exit",
104	    )
105	    p.add_argument(
106	        "--test-mode", action="store_true", dest="test_mode",
107	        help="read a regular file instead of a block device (rootless self-test)",
108	    )
109	    imgcfg.add_arguments(p, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)
110	    return p.parse_args()
111	
112	
113	def main() -> int:
114	    """Resolve the plan, print it, then capture unless --dry-run."""
115	    args = parse_args()
116	    if args.list_devices:
117	        return devsafe.list_devices()
118	    file_cfg = imgcfg.load_config(args.config, SECTIONS, DEFAULTS)
119	    plan = imgcfg.resolve(args, file_cfg, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)
120	    print_plan(args.config, plan)
121	
122	    if args.dry_run:
123	        return 0
124	    return run_capture(plan, test_mode=args.test_mode)
125	
126	
127	if __name__ == "__main__":
128	    raise SystemExit(main())
129
```

```
1	"""Write a deployable image to a target device — the write app.
2	
3	`write` is one of the three image-builder apps (`build`, `capture`,
4	`write`) and the destructive one: it writes `[image].output` to a
5	physical block device. Writing a device needs root, so the `dd` runs
6	under `sudo`; the surrounding targeting + inspection stays rootless.
7	
8	`write` is the read/write counterpart of `capture` — capture reads a
9	device into an image, write writes an image to a device. Because a wrong
10	target is unrecoverable, write runs the full `devsafe` gate before any
11	write:
12	
13	- two-key targeting — the caller names the device twice, `--target`
14	  (`/dev/xx`) and `--target-byid` (`/dev/disk/by-id/yy`); they must
15	  resolve to the same major:minor or it aborts,
16	- system-disk refusal — any target backing a mounted filesystem is
17	  refused outright,
18	- two-factor confirm — an interactive confirm, skippable only with BOTH
19	  `[write].allow_yes` (config) and `--yes` (per run).
20	
21	Expand-to-fill (grow the rootfs to fill the device) lands in 0.3.0-5.4.2.
22	`--test-mode` writes to a regular file and skips the device gate, so the
23	write path is exercisable against a built image with no root or hardware.
24	`--dry-run` resolves and prints the plan, touching nothing.
25	
26	    # list candidate devices (with by-id), then exit
27	    uv run python scripts/write.py --list-devices
28	
29	    # write the deploy image to a device (needs sudo)
30	    uv run python scripts/write.py \\
31	        --target /dev/sdb --target-byid /dev/disk/by-id/usb-...
32	
33	    # exercise the write path against a file, no root
34	    uv run python scripts/write.py --test-mode \\
35	        --output images/fc-deploy.img --target images/_test-card.img
36	"""
37	
38	import argparse
39	from pathlib import Path
40	
41	import devsafe
42	import imgcfg
43	
44	# fc-config.toml tables this app reads: shared artifact paths + write's
45	# own knobs.
46	SECTIONS = ("image", "write")
47	
48	# Built-in fallback mirroring the committed [image] + [write] defaults.
49	# `allow_yes` is here but absent from STR_KEYS / BOOL_KEYS, so argparse
50	# generates no `--allow-yes` — the confirm-bypass permission is grantable
51	# only in the config, and skipping also needs `--yes` (two-factor).
52	DEFAULTS: dict[str, object] = {
53	    "images_dir": "images",
54	    "output": "fc-deploy.img",
55	    "target": "",
56	    "target_byid": "",
57	    "expand": True,
58	    "allow_yes": False,
59	}
60	
61	STR_KEYS = ("images_dir", "output", "target", "target_byid")
62	BOOL_KEYS = ("expand",)
63	
64	
65	def print_plan(config_path: Path, plan: dict) -> None:
66	    """Print the resolved settings and the write stage."""
67	    imgcfg.print_settings(config_path, plan, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS, "write")
68	    output = imgcfg.img_path(plan, "output")
69	    target = plan["target"][0]
70	    print("")
71	    print("stages:")
72	    if target:
73	        print(f"  1. write    dd if={output} of={target}")
74	        if plan["expand"][0]:
75	            print("  2. expand   grow root partition + resize2fs to fill the device")
76	    else:
77	        print("  1. write    (no target set — nothing to write)")
78	
79	
80	def run_write(plan: dict, *, allow_yes: bool, yes_flag: bool, test_mode: bool) -> int:
81	    """Write the output image to the target device behind the safety gate.
82	
83	    - real mode: two-key targeting + system-disk refusal + interactive
84	      confirm, then `sudo dd` the output image to the device,
85	    - `test_mode`: skip the device gate (it needs a real block device,
86	      validated separately) and write to a file, so the path is
87	      self-testable,
88	    - then, unless `[write].expand` is off, grow the root partition +
89	      filesystem to fill the device.
90	    """
91	    output = imgcfg.img_path(plan, "output")
92	    target = plan["target"][0]
93	    byid = plan["target_byid"][0]
94	    if not target:
95	        raise SystemExit("write: no --target device given (nothing to write)")
96	    if not output.exists():
97	        raise SystemExit(f"write: image not found: {output} (run `just build` first)")
98	
99	    if test_mode:
100	        dev = devsafe.resolve_device(target, test_mode=True)
101	    else:
102	        if not byid:
103	            raise SystemExit(
104	                "write: --target requires --target-byid (the /dev/disk/by-id handle)")
105	        dev = devsafe.verify_two_key(target, byid)
106	        devsafe.assert_safe_target(dev)
107	        devsafe.confirm(dev, "write", allow_yes=allow_yes, yes_flag=yes_flag)
108	
109	    print(f"\nwrite: {output} -> {dev}{' (test-mode)' if test_mode else ''}")
110	    devsafe.dd_write(output, dev, test_mode=test_mode)
111	    if plan["expand"][0]:
112	        devsafe.expand_to_device(dev, test_mode=test_mode)
113	    print("\nwrite: image -> device complete.")
114	    return 0
115	
116	
117	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
118	    """Build the parser: control flags + one flag per [image]/[write] key."""
119	    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(
120	        prog="write",
121	        description=__doc__.splitlines()[0],
122	    )
123	    p.add_argument(
124	        "--config", type=Path, default=imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG,
125	        help=f"fc-config.toml to read [image]/[write] from (default: {imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG})",
126	    )
127	    p.add_argument(
128	        "--dry-run", action="store_true", dest="dry_run",
129	        help="resolve and print the plan, mutate nothing",
130	    )
131	    p.add_argument(
132	        "--list-devices", action="store_true", dest="list_devices",
133	        help="list block devices (with by-id) for picking a target, then exit",
134	    )
135	    p.add_argument(
136	        "--test-mode", action="store_true", dest="test_mode",
137	        help="write a regular file and skip the device gate (rootless self-test)",
138	    )
139	    p.add_argument(
140	        "--yes", action="store_true", dest="yes",
141	        help="skip the write confirm — only with [write].allow_yes=true (else errors)",
142	    )
143	    imgcfg.add_arguments(p, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)
144	    return p.parse_args()
145	
146	
147	def main() -> int:
148	    """Resolve the plan, print it, then write unless --dry-run."""
149	    args = parse_args()
150	    if args.list_devices:
151	        return devsafe.list_devices()
152	    file_cfg = imgcfg.load_config(args.config, SECTIONS, DEFAULTS)
153	    plan = imgcfg.resolve(args, file_cfg, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)
154	    print_plan(args.config, plan)
155	
156	    if args.dry_run:
157	        return 0
158	    return run_write(plan, allow_yes=bool(file_cfg["allow_yes"]),
159	                     yes_flag=args.yes, test_mode=args.test_mode)
160	
161	
162	if __name__ == "__main__":
163	    raise SystemExit(main())
164
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/nps-gnc/bdimager/src/bdimager/capture.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Capture a source block device into the mother image — the capture app.\n\n`capture` is one of the three image-builder apps (`build`, `capture`,\n`write`). It reads a source block device — an SD card, USB stick, NVMe,\nor disk — into `[image].mother` (via the device layer, `devsafe`) so a\nsubsequent `build` can transform it. Reading a block device needs root,\nso the `dd` runs under `sudo`; the surrounding inspection (device\nresolution, `--list-devices`) stays rootless.\n\ncapture is a *non-destructive read*, so it takes a single source device\n(`--source`, a `/dev/xx` node or a `/dev/disk/by-id/yy` handle) — no\ntwo-key targeting and no confirm (that safety guards the destructive\nwrite, not this). `--test-mode` reads a regular file instead of a block\ndevice, so the capture path is exercisable against a synthetic\nmk_test_image `.img` with no root or hardware.\n\nSettings come from `bd-config.toml`'s `[image]` (shared artifact paths)\nand `[capture]` (this app's `source`); see `imgcfg` for the config / env\n/ CLI precedence. `--dry-run` resolves and prints the plan, touching\nnothing.\n\n    # list candidate devices (with by-id), then exit\n    bd-capture --list-devices\n\n    # capture a device into the mother (needs sudo)\n    bd-capture --source /dev/sdb\n\n    # exercise the capture path against a synthetic file, no root\n    bd-capture --test-mode \\\\\n        --source images/_test-card.img --mother images/_test-mother.img\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom . import devsafe, imgcfg\n\n# bd-config.toml tables this app reads: shared artifact paths + capture's\n# own knobs.\nSECTIONS = (\"image\", \"capture\")\n\n# Built-in fallback mirroring the committed [image] + [capture] defaults,\n# so the tool resolves a complete plan against a stripped config.\nDEFAULTS: dict[str, object] = {\n    \"images_dir\": \"images\",\n    \"mother\": \"mother.img\",\n    \"source\": \"\",\n}\n\nSTR_KEYS = (\"images_dir\", \"mother\", \"source\")\nBOOL_KEYS = ()\n\n\ndef print_plan(config_path: Path, plan: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Print the resolved settings and the capture stage.\"\"\"\n    imgcfg.print_settings(config_path, plan, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS, \"capture\")\n    source = plan[\"source\"][0]\n    mother = imgcfg.img_path(plan, \"mother\")\n    print(\"\")\n    print(\"stages:\")\n    if source:\n        print(f\"  1. capture   dd if={source} of={mother}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  1. capture   (no source set — nothing to capture)\")\n\n\ndef run_capture(plan: dict, *, test_mode: bool) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Capture the source device into the mother image.\n\n    - resolves the single `--source` handle (`/dev/xx` or by-id) to a\n      device, then copies it into `[image].mother` via devsafe,\n    - `test_mode` reads a regular file instead of a block device so the\n      path is self-testable; otherwise the read runs under `sudo dd`.\n    \"\"\"\n    source = plan[\"source\"][0]\n    if not source:\n        raise SystemExit(\"capture: no --source device given (nothing to capture)\")\n    mother = imgcfg.img_path(plan, \"mother\")\n    dev = devsafe.resolve_device(source, test_mode=test_mode)\n    print(f\"\\ncapture: {dev} -> {mother}{' (test-mode)' if test_mode else ''}\")\n    devsafe.dd_capture(dev, mother, test_mode=test_mode)\n    print(\"\\ncapture: device -> mother complete.\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:\n    \"\"\"Build the parser: control flags + one flag per [image]/[capture] key.\"\"\"\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        prog=\"bd-capture\",\n        description=__doc__.splitlines()[0],\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--config\", type=Path, default=imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n        help=f\"bd-config.toml to read [image]/[capture] from (default: {imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG})\",\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"dry_run\",\n        help=\"resolve and print the plan, mutate nothing\",\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--list-devices\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"list_devices\",\n        help=\"list block devices (with by-id) for picking a source, then exit\",\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--test-mode\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"test_mode\",\n        help=\"read a regular file instead of a block device (rootless self-test)\",\n    )\n    imgcfg.add_arguments(p, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the plan, print it, then capture unless --dry-run.\"\"\"\n    args = parse_args()\n    if args.list_devices:\n        return devsafe.list_devices()\n    file_cfg = imgcfg.load_config(args.config, SECTIONS, DEFAULTS)\n    plan = imgcfg.resolve(args, file_cfg, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)\n    print_plan(args.config, plan)\n\n    if args.dry_run:\n        return 0\n    return run_capture(plan, test_mode=args.test_mode)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n"
}
```

```json
  "content": "\"\"\"Write a deployable image to a target device — the write app.\n\n`write` is one of the three image-builder apps (`build`, `capture`,\n`write`) and the destructive one: it writes `[image].output` to a\nphysical block device. Writing a device needs root, so the `dd` runs\nunder `sudo`; the surrounding targeting + inspection stays rootless.\n\n`write` is the read/write counterpart of `capture` — capture reads a\ndevice into an image, write writes an image to a device. Because a wrong\ntarget is unrecoverable, write runs the full `devsafe` gate before any\nwrite:\n\n- two-key targeting — the caller names the device twice, `--target`\n  (`/dev/xx`) and `--target-byid` (`/dev/disk/by-id/yy`); they must\n  resolve to the same major:minor or it aborts,\n- system-disk refusal — any target backing a mounted filesystem is\n  refused outright,\n- two-factor confirm — an interactive confirm, skippable only with BOTH\n  `[write].allow_yes` (config) and `--yes` (per run).\n\nUnless `[write].expand` is off, after the write the root partition and\nits filesystem are grown to fill the target (the shrunk image leaves the\nrest of the device unused). `--test-mode` writes to a regular file and\nskips the device gate, so the write + expand path is exercisable against\na built image with no root or hardware. `--dry-run` resolves and prints\nthe plan, touching nothing.\n\n    # list candidate devices (with by-id), then exit\n    bd-write --list-devices\n\n    # write the deploy image to a device (needs sudo)\n    bd-write --target /dev/sdb --target-byid /dev/disk/by-id/usb-...\n\n    # exercise the write path against a file, no root\n    bd-write --test-mode \\\\\n        --output images/deploy.img --target images/_test-card.img\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom . import devsafe, imgcfg\n\n# bd-config.toml tables this app reads: shared artifact paths + write's\n# own knobs.\nSECTIONS = (\"image\", \"write\")\n\n# Built-in fallback mirroring the committed [image] + [write] defaults.\n# `allow_yes` is here but absent from STR_KEYS / BOOL_KEYS, so argparse\n# generates no `--allow-yes` — the confirm-bypass permission is grantable\n# only in the config, and skipping also needs `--yes` (two-factor).\nDEFAULTS: dict[str, object] = {\n    \"images_dir\": \"images\",\n    \"output\": \"deploy.img\",\n    \"target\": \"\",\n    \"target_byid\": \"\",\n    \"expand\": True,\n    \"allow_yes\": False,\n}\n\nSTR_KEYS = (\"images_dir\", \"output\", \"target\", \"target_byid\")\nBOOL_KEYS = (\"expand\",)\n\n\ndef print_plan(config_path: Path, plan: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Print the resolved settings and the write stage.\"\"\"\n    imgcfg.print_settings(config_path, plan, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS, \"write\")\n    output = imgcfg.img_path(plan, \"output\")\n    target = plan[\"target\"][0]\n    print(\"\")\n    print(\"stages:\")\n    if target:\n        print(f\"  1. write    dd if={output} of={target}\")\n        if plan[\"expand\"][0]:\n            print(\"  2. expand   grow root partition + resize2fs to fill the device\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  1. write    (no target set — nothing to write)\")\n\n\ndef run_write(plan: dict, *, allow_yes: bool, yes_flag: bool, test_mode: bool) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Write the output image to the target device behind the safety gate.\n\n    - real mode: two-key targeting + system-disk refusal + interactive\n      confirm, then `sudo dd` the output image to the device,\n    - `test_mode`: skip the device gate (it needs a real block device,\n      validated separately) and write to a file, so the path is\n      self-testable,\n    - then, unless `[write].expand` is off, grow the root partition +\n      filesystem to fill the device.\n    \"\"\"\n    output = imgcfg.img_path(plan, \"output\")\n    target = plan[\"target\"][0]\n    byid = plan[\"target_byid\"][0]\n    if not target:\n        raise SystemExit(\"write: no --target device given (nothing to write)\")\n    if not output.exists():\n        raise SystemExit(f\"write: image not found: {output} (run bd-build first)\")\n\n    if test_mode:\n        dev = devsafe.resolve_device(target, test_mode=True)\n    else:\n        if not byid:\n            raise SystemExit(\n                \"write: --target requires --target-byid (the /dev/disk/by-id handle)\")\n        dev = devsafe.verify_two_key(target, byid)\n        devsafe.assert_safe_target(dev)\n        devsafe.confirm(dev, \"write\", allow_yes=allow_yes, yes_flag=yes_flag)\n\n    print(f\"\\nwrite: {output} -> {dev}{' (test-mode)' if test_mode else ''}\")\n    devsafe.dd_write(output, dev, test_mode=test_mode)\n    if plan[\"expand\"][0]:\n        devsafe.expand_to_device(dev, test_mode=test_mode)\n    print(\"\\nwrite: image -> device complete.\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:\n    \"\"\"Build the parser: control flags + one flag per [image]/[write] key.\"\"\"\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        prog=\"bd-write\",\n        description=__doc__.splitlines()[0],\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--config\", type=Path, default=imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG,\n        help=f\"bd-config.toml to read [image]/[write] from (default: {imgcfg.DEFAULT_CONFIG})\",\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"dry_run\",\n        help=\"resolve and print the plan, mutate nothing\",\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--list-devices\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"list_devices\",\n        help=\"list block devices (with by-id) for picking a target, then exit\",\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--test-mode\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"test_mode\",\n        help=\"write a regular file and skip the device gate (rootless self-test)\",\n    )\n    p.add_argument(\n        \"--yes\", action=\"store_true\", dest=\"yes\",\n        help=\"skip the write confirm — only with [write].allow_yes=true (else errors)\",\n    )\n    imgcfg.add_arguments(p, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the plan, print it, then write unless --dry-run.\"\"\"\n    args = parse_args()\n    if args.list_devices:\n        return devsafe.list_devices()\n    file_cfg = imgcfg.load_config(args.config, SECTIONS, DEFAULTS)\n    plan = imgcfg.resolve(args, file_cfg, STR_KEYS, BOOL_KEYS)\n    print_plan(args.config, plan)\n\n    if args.dry_run:\n        return 0\n    return run_write(plan, allow_yes=bool(file_cfg[\"allow_yes\"]),\n                     yes_flag=args.yes, ```json
```
```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/nps-gnc/bdimager/tests/test_devices.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Tests for the device apps (0.3.0-3): capture and write driven in\n--test-mode (regular files standing in for block devices), so the full\ncapture -> build -> write -> expand chain runs rootless, no hardware.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom bdimager import build, capture, imglib, mk_test_image, write\n\n\ndef _build_plan(mother, output, *, shrink=True, compress=False):\n    return {\n        \"images_dir\": (\"images\", \"config\"),\n        \"mother\": (str(mother), \"flag\"),\n        \"output\": (str(output), \"flag\"),\n        \"shrink\": (shrink, \"flag\"),\n        \"compress\": (compress, \"flag\"),\n    }\n\n\ndef test_capture_then_build(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"capture --test-mode copies a synthetic 'card' into the mother, then build.\"\"\"\n    card = tmp_path / \"card.img\"\n    mother = tmp_path / \"mother.img\"\n    deploy = tmp_path / \"deploy.img\"\n    assert mk_test_image.build(card) == 0\n\n    plan = {\n        \"images_dir\": (\"images\", \"config\"),\n        \"mother\": (str(mother), \"flag\"),\n        \"source\": (str(card), \"flag\"),\n    }\n    assert capture.run_capture(plan, test_mode=True) == 0\n    assert mother.exists()\n    assert mother.read_bytes() == card.read_bytes()\n\n    # The captured mother builds into a valid deployable image.\n    assert build.run_build(_build_plan(mother, deploy, compress=True), []) == 0\n    assert mk_test_image.verify(deploy) == 0\n\n\ndef test_write_then_expand(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"write --test-mode writes a deploy image into a larger 'card', then expands.\"\"\"\n    mother = tmp_path / \"mother.img\"\n    deploy = tmp_path / \"deploy.img\"\n    card = tmp_path / \"card.img\"\n    assert mk_test_image.build(mother) == 0\n    # Shrink so the deploy is smaller than the card, leaving room to expand.\n    assert build.run_build(_build_plan(mother, deploy, shrink=True), []) == 0\n\n    # Pre-size the card larger than the deploy image (a real card would be).\n    with open(card, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.truncate(mk_test_image.TOTAL_BYTES)\n\n    plan = {\n        \"images_dir\": (\"images\", \"config\"),\n        \"output\": (str(deploy), \"flag\"),\n        \"target\": (str(card), \"flag\"),\n        \"target_byid\": (\"\", \"config\"),\n        \"expand\": (True, \"flag\"),\n        \"allow_yes\": (False, \"config\"),\n    }\n    assert write.run_write(plan, allow_yes=False, yes_flag=False, test_mode=True) == 0\n\n    # The root partition + rootfs grew to fill the card, survivor intact.\n    assert mk_test_image.verify_expanded(card) == 0\n    _boot, root, _parts = imglib.find_boot_root(card)\n    root_fs = imglib.extract_region(card, root[\"off\"], root[\"length\"])\n    try:\n        assert imglib.fs_read(root_fs, mk_test_image.SURVIVOR_PATH) == \\\n            mk_test_image.SURVIVOR_CONTENT\n    finally:\n        root_fs.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/nps-gnc/bdimager/README.md",
  "old_string": "When complete this Python tool will allow you to take a working\nimage from a block device, typically an sd-card, and\n- Capture the contents to a file *mother* image\n- Build this into a distributable(?) image\n- Write the distributable image to a target block device\n  and this target device can be a different size than\n  the *mother* image.\n\nEventually I'd like to convert this to Rust.",
  "new_string": "A Python tool to image block devices (typically SD cards) through\nthree commands:\n\n- `bd-capture` — copy a source block device to a file *mother* image.\n- `bd-build` — turn the mother into a distributable image: inject\n  caller-specified files/directories, then shrink the rootfs and xz\n  it.\n- `bd-write` — write the distributable image to a target block\n  device, growing the rootfs to fill a target larger than the image.\n\nThe image-editing path is **rootless**: `bd-build` edits the\nfilesystems *inside* the `.img` (`debugfs` for ext, `mtools` for\nFAT) rather than loop-mounting, so no `sudo` is needed. Only the\nphysical-device `dd` of capture/write needs root.\n\nEventually I'd like to convert this to Rust.\n\n## Requirements\n\n- Python ≥ 3.14 and [`uv`](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/).\n- System tools on `PATH`: `sfdisk` / `lsblk` (util-linux),\n  `debugfs` / `mke2fs` / `resize2fs` / `dumpe2fs` / `e2fsck`\n  (e2fsprogs), `mformat` / `mcopy` / `mdir` (mtools), and `xz`.\n\n## Usage\n\nSettings come from a `bd-config.toml` in the current directory\n(`[image]` shared paths plus `[build]` / `[capture]` / `[write]`\nknobs). Precedence is lowest to highest: config file < `BD_<KEY>`\nenv var < CLI flag. Every command takes `--dry-run` to resolve and\nprint the plan without touching anything, and `--config` to point\nat another file.\n\n### bd-capture (device → mother)\n\nA non-destructive read; needs `sudo` for the real `dd`.\n\n```\nbd-capture --list-devices                  # pick a source (shows by-id)\nbd-capture --source /dev/sdb               # capture into the mother\nbd-capture --test-mode --source card.img --mother mother.img  # rootless\n```\n\n### bd-build (mother → deployable image)\n\nFully rootless. Injection places host files/directories into the\nimage rootfs:\n\n- `--add SRC:DEST` or `--add SRC->DEST` — copy host `SRC` (a file\n  or a directory tree) to absolute image path `DEST`. Repeatable.\n- the separator is `:` or `->`; per entry pick whichever your\n  paths don't contain — e.g. use `->` when the destination has a\n  colon (`'./a->/etc/wei:rd'`). `DEST` must be absolute; an entry\n  that parses validly under *both* separators is a hard error.\n- an existing `DEST` is overwritten (its mode/uid/gid preserved);\n  overwrites are listed in the plan.\n- the same arguments may live in a `@file` — one `--add SRC:DEST`\n  per line, `#` comments allowed — passed as `bd-build @adds.txt`.\n\n```\nbd-build --mother mother.img --output deploy.img \\\n    --add ./app.conf:/etc/app.conf --add ./assets:/opt/app/assets\nbd-build --no-shrink --no-compress         # skip the finalize steps\n```\n\n### bd-write (image → device)\n\nDestructive, so it runs the full safety gate: two-key targeting\n(`--target` plus the `--target-byid` `/dev/disk/by-id` handle, which\nmust resolve to the same device), refusal of any disk backing a\nmounted filesystem, and an interactive confirm. The confirm is\nskippable only with both `[write].allow_yes = true` and `--yes`.\nAfter writing, the rootfs is grown to fill the target unless\n`--no-expand`.\n\n```\nbd-write --list-devices\nbd-write --target /dev/sdb --target-byid /dev/disk/by-id/usb-...\nbd-write --test-mode --output deploy.img --target card.img  # rootless\n```"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/nps-gnc/bdimager/ARCHITECTURE.md",
  "old_string": "# Architecture\n\nThis file uses [Prose form](AGENTS.md#prose-form). It will\ndefine how this code is structured — a stub for now (no code\nyet); the See-also links below are the current content.",
  "new_string": "# Architecture\n\nThis file uses [Prose form](AGENTS.md#prose-form). It describes\nhow the `bdimager` package is structured.\n\n## Pipeline\n\nThree standalone apps move an image through two artifacts — a\n*mother* image and a *deployable* image:\n\n- **capture** (`bd-capture`) reads a source block device into the\n  mother image.\n- **build** (`bd-build`) transforms the mother into the deployable\n  image — inject files/dirs, shrink the rootfs, xz it.\n- **write** (`bd-write`) writes the deployable image to a target\n  device and grows the rootfs to fill it.\n\n`build` is fully rootless; only `capture` / `write` touch a\nphysical device (the `dd`, under `sudo`).\n\n## Modules (`src/bdimager/`)\n\n- **`imglib.py`** — rootless in-file storage toolkit. Locates\n  partitions with `sfdisk -J`, edits the ext root with `debugfs`\n  (extract the partition to a temp file at offset 0, edit, splice\n  it back), edits the FAT boot with `mtools` (`image@@offset`),\n  and resizes with `resize2fs`. No loop-mount, no root.\n- **`imgcfg.py`** — shared config plumbing. Loads `bd-config.toml`\n  tables, merges file < `BD_*` env < CLI flag into a resolved\n  plan, generates the per-key flags, and prints the plan.\n- **`devsafe.py`** — physical-device targeting + safety (two-key\n  match, system-disk refusal, content preview, confirm) and the\n  privileged `dd` capture/write plus expand. The one module that\n  touches a device.\n- **`build.py`** — the rootless build app: transform copy, inject\n  (`--add` / `@file`), finalize (shrink + xz). Imports only\n  `imglib` + `imgcfg`, never `devsafe`, so \"build is rootless\"\n  holds structurally.\n- **`capture.py`** / **`write.py`** — the device apps, over\n  `devsafe` + `imgcfg`.\n- **`mk_test_image.py`** — builds / verifies a small synthetic\n  Pi-like image so the build + device paths self-test rootlessly\n  (no card, no root); used by the `tests/`.\n\n## Dependency direction\n\n`imglib` and `imgcfg` are leaves. `devsafe` → `imglib`. `build` →\n`imglib` + `imgcfg`. `capture` / `write` → `devsafe` + `imgcfg`.\n`mk_test_image` → `imglib`."
}
```

```json
  "content": "# bdimager recipes — run `just --list` to see them.\n#\n# The image-editing recipes (build and the *-test smokes) are rootless:\n# they edit the filesystems inside the .img with debugfs / mtools, no\n# sudo. Only capture / write to a real block device need root.\n\n# Show available recipes.\n_default:\n    @just --list\n\n# Run the test suite (pass pytest args, e.g. `just test -k build`).\ntest *args:\n    uv run pytest {{args}}\n\n# Build a deployable image from a mother (rootless). Settings from\n# bd-config.toml [image]/[build]; pass --add <src>:<dest>, --dry-run, ...\nbuild *args:\n    uv run bd-build {{args}}\n\n# Capture a source block device into the mother (needs sudo for the read).\n# Settings from bd-config.toml [image]/[capture]; --list-devices to pick.\ncapture *args:\n    uv run bd-capture {{args}}\n\n# Write a deployable image to a target device (DESTRUCTIVE; needs sudo).\n# Settings from bd-config.toml [image]/[write]; --list-devices to pick.\nwrite *args:\n    uv run bd-write {{args}}\n\n# Build / verify a synthetic test image (rootless): build / verify /\n# verify-expanded <img>.\nmk-test-image *args:\n    uv run python -m bdimager.mk_test_image {{args}}\n\n# Self-test the build app rootlessly (no sudo, no hardware): synthesize a\n# mother, build + shrink + compress, verify. Throwaway images removed.\nimage-test:\n    #!/usr/bin/env bash\n    set -euo pipefail\n    m=images/_test-mother.img; d=images/_test-deploy.img\n    trap 'rm -f \"$m\" \"$d\" \"$d\".xz' EXIT\n    uv run python -m bdimager.mk_test_image build \"$m\"\n    uv run bd-build --mother \"$m\" --output \"$d\" --shrink --compress\n    uv run python -m bdimager.mk_test_image verify \"$d\"\n\n# Self-test the capture app rootlessly (no sudo, no card): --test-mode\n# \"captures\" a synthetic card into the mother, then build + verify.\ncapture-test:\n    #!/usr/bin/env bash\n    set -euo pipefail\n    card=images/_test-card.img; m=images/_test-mother.img; d=images/_test-deploy.img\n    trap 'rm -f \"$card\" \"$m\" \"$d\" \"$d\".xz' EXIT\n    uv run python -m bdimager.mk_test_image build \"$card\"\n    uv run bd-capture --test-mode --source \"$card\" --mother \"$m\"\n    uv run bd-build --mother \"$m\" --output \"$d\" --shrink --compress\n    uv run python -m bdimager.mk_test_image verify \"$d\"\n\n# Self-test the write app rootlessly (no sudo, no device): --test-mode\n# writes a built deploy image into a card file padded larger than the\n# image, then expands to fill it; verify the partition + rootfs grew.\nwrite-test:\n    #!/usr/bin/env bash\n    set -euo pipefail\n    m=images/_test-mother.img; d=images/_test-deploy.img; card=images/_test-card.img\n    trap 'rm -f \"$m\" \"$d\" \"$d\".xz \"$card\"' EXIT\n    uv run python -m bdimager.mk_test_image build \"$m\"\n    uv run bd-build --mother \"$m\" --output \"$d\" --shrink\n    truncate -s 160M \"$card\"\n    uv run bd-write --test-mode --output \"$d\" --target \"$card\"\n    uv run python -m bdimager.mk_test_image verify-expanded \"$card\"\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/nps-gnc/bdimager/notes/chores/chores-01.md",
  "content": "# Chores-01\n\nChores-XX files use [Prose form](../../AGENTS.md#prose-form). They\ncontain discussions and notes on various chores in github compatible\nmarkdown. There is also a [todo.md](../todo.md) file that tracks\ntasks and in general there should be a chore section for each task\nwith the why and how this task will be completed.\n\n## chore: scaffold package + open cycle\n\nCommits: [[1]]\n\nStood up bdimager as an installable package — uv + hatchling, the\nversion-of-record in `bd-config.toml` `[project].version` (PEP 440\n`+` form of the cycle version), and `bd-capture` / `bd-build` /\n`bd-write` console scripts. Opened the 0.3.0 cycle that ports fc's\nSD-card image builder into bdimager, generalized.\n\n## feat: rootless storage + config core\n\nCommits: [[2]]\n\nPorted fc's rootless storage toolkit and config plumbing,\nde-fc'd:\n\n- `imglib` — locate partitions with `sfdisk`, edit the ext root\n  with `debugfs` (extract / splice), the FAT boot with `mtools`,\n  resize with `resize2fs`; no loop-mount, no root.\n- `imgcfg` — load `bd-config.toml` tables and merge\n  file < `BD_*` env < CLI into a resolved plan. The `[build]`\n  schema was cut to the generic `shrink` / `compress`; fc's\n  `fc_path` / `autologin` / `scrub_*` keys are gone.\n- `devsafe` and `mk_test_image` ported alongside, the latter\n  reseeded with generic anchors (a survivor file + a stale\n  overwrite target) in place of fc's scrub/bake fixtures.\n\n## feat: build app + generic file/dir injection\n\nCommits: [[3]]\n\nThe build app, generalized. fc's secret-scrub and app-baking\n(config / inittab / .profile / local.d) were dropped; the\nrootless transform -> inject -> finalize pipeline now injects\ncaller-specified files and directories instead. The `[build]`\nconfig schema is just `shrink` / `compress`.\n\n### Injection design\n\n- `--add SRC<sep>DEST` copies a host file or directory tree to an\n  absolute image path; repeatable, and the same lines work from a\n  `@file` (argparse fromfile, shlex-split, `#` comments).\n- The separator is `:` or `->`. Per entry an unambiguous split is\n  chosen by the absolute-DEST rule: split by each separator the\n  entry contains, keep a result only if DEST is absolute and both\n  sides are non-empty. Exactly one valid split wins (so a colon\n  inside a `->` dest is fine); zero is a format error; two\n  distinct valid splits are ambiguous and rejected. The two\n  separators are thus mutual escape hatches for a path that\n  contains the other.\n- An existing DEST is overwritten with its mode / uid / gid\n  preserved; a fresh file takes the source mode and root:root.\n  Overwrites are listed in the plan.\n\n## feat: capture + write device apps\n\nCommits: [[4]]\n\nPorted the two device apps over `devsafe`:\n\n- `capture` — a single-source, non-destructive read of a block\n  device into the mother (no two-key / confirm; that guards the\n  write).\n- `write` — the full gate (two-key targeting, system-disk\n  refusal, two-factor confirm), then expand the rootfs to fill\n  the target.\n- both take `--test-mode` (a regular file stands in for a device),\n  so the capture -> build -> write -> expand chain self-tests\n  rootless.\n\n## docs: README + ARCHITECTURE + deferred todo\n\nCommits: [[5]]\n\nREADME rewritten to the implemented three-command tool (usage,\nthe `--add` / `@file` syntax, the rootless note, the `bd-write`\nsafety gate); ARCHITECTURE filled with the pipeline + module map;\nthe deferred installable-packages work captured as a Todo\n(tier-two: real package installation needs mount + chroot +\nqemu-user, not the rootless debugfs path).\n\n## chore: add justfile\n\nCommits: [[6]]\n\nAdded a `justfile` wrapping the apps, `pytest`, and the rootless\nimage-test / capture-test / write-test smokes over the installed\n`bd-*` console scripts — to cut the bare `uv run ...` friction\nseen during the cycle.\n\n## chore: close out 0.3.0 image-builder port\n\nCommits:\n\nClose-out of the 0.3.0 cycle — bookkeeping only: move the In\nProgress block here, record the Done entry, and backfill the\n`Commits:` refs above (the prior commits are now permanent on\n`main`).\n\n### Cycle decisions\n\n- Generalize, no fc-isms: `bd-config.toml`, `BD_*` env,\n  `bd-*` console scripts; fc's secret-scrub and app-baking\n  dropped.\n- A proper installable package (`src/bdimager/`, pyproject +\n  hatchling).\n- build injection is caller-driven — see the\n  [Injection design](#injection-design) above.\n- KISS scope: file/dir copy on the rootless `debugfs` path only;\n  proper package *installation* is deferred to a Todo.\n\n### As-built ladder\n\n- 0.3.0-0 chore: scaffold package + open cycle [[1]]\n- 0.3.0-1 feat: rootless storage + config core [[2]]\n- 0.3.0-2 feat: build app + generic file/dir injection [[3]]\n- 0.3.0-3 feat: capture + write device apps [[4]]\n- 0.3.0-4 docs: README + ARCHITECTURE + deferred todo [[5]]\n- 0.3.0-5 chore: add justfile [[6]]\n- 0.3.0 close-out (this commit)\n\n### Outcome\n\n18 pytest tests pass rootless (debugfs / mtools / sfdisk under\nthe hood); the full capture -> build -> write -> expand chain\nself-tests with no root or hardware, and the `just` smokes run\nthe same flow through the installed console scripts. The\nreal-device write path (sudo `dd` + sfdisk/resize2fs on hardware)\nis not exercised in CI.\n\n# References\n\n[1]: https://github.com/winksaville/bdimager/commit/1c3e9a51b3b7 \"1c3e9a51b3b768e13f9da04101c55902b4c8bb24\"\n[2]: https://github.com/winksaville/bdimager/commit/dc5adc0f4a42 \"dc5adc0f4a425916fd599411a2b674713362e019\"\n[3]: https://github.com/winksaville/bdimager/commit/a6d6806d5345 \"a6d6806d5345740d64465fdd77434ce62e715b4e\"\n[4]: https://github.com/winksaville/bdimager/commit/5b8e2a2299b8 \"5b8e2a2299b807dbf97d3ab23405e6e4acf8db54\"\n[5]: https://github.com/winksaville/bdimager/commit/060bdca2248e \"060bdca2248e512e0309ab76122ae7079397687e\"\n[6]: https://github.com/winksaville/bdimager/commit/9cdf1ead6f0e \"9cdf1ead6f0efde9e58dcb9b62c1034bf4492904\"\n"
```

```
1	# Todo
2	
3	This file uses [Prose form](../AGENTS.md#prose-form). It
4	contains near term tasks with a short description and
5	uses links or reference links for more details.
6	
7	## In Progress
8	
9	**Port fc's image builder into bdimager (generalized)**
10	
11	bdimager is an empty scaffold. fc has a working rootless SD-card
12	image builder (capture → build → write, ~2,000 lines across 7
13	modules) that maps onto bdimager's purpose. Port it in as a
14	proper installable Python package, stripped of all fc-isms,
15	replacing fc's app-baking with a generic file/dir injection
16	feature.
17	
18	Decisions:
19	
20	- Generalize, no fc-isms: `bd-config.toml`, `BD_*` env prefix,
21	  `bd-capture` / `bd-build` / `bd-write` console scripts.
22	- Proper installable package: `src/bdimager/` + `pyproject.toml`.
23	- `build` injection: explicit `src`→`dest`, accepting **both**
24	  `:` and `->` (mutual collision escape hatch); an entry
25	  ambiguous in *both* separators is a hard error;
26	  dest-already-exists → overwrite, surfaced in `--dry-run`;
27	  supplied via `--add` on the CLI or via a `@file` (argparse
28	  fromfile).
29	- Scope KISS: file/dir copy only, on fc's rootless
30	  `debugfs` / `mtools` path (no mount). Real package
31	  installation is deferred to a todo (tier-two: needs
32	  mount + chroot + qemu-user, not the rootless debugfs path).
33	
34	Ladder:
35	
36	- 0.3.0-0 chore: scaffold package + open cycle (done)
37	  - `pyproject.toml` (version-of-record `0.3.0+0`, PEP 440
38	    `-`→`+`; hatchling; the three console scripts),
39	    `src/bdimager/` skeleton, uv / `.python-version`.
40	  - Move this item into `## In Progress`; open the chores-01
41	    section (empty `Commits:`).
42	- 0.3.0-1 feat: rootless storage + config core (done)
43	  - Port `imglib`, `imgcfg`, `devsafe`, `mk_test_image` into the
44	    package, de-fc'd.
45	  - `bd-config.toml` schema: drop fc build keys (`fc_path`,
46	    `fc_config_target`, `autologin_tty`, `scrub_*`), keep the
47	    generic `image` / `capture` / `write` / `build` keys, add
48	    the inject key; `BD_*` env.
49	  - Validate: `debugfs` / `mtools` round-trip smoke via
50	    `mk_test_image`.
51	- 0.3.0-2 feat: build app + generic file/dir injection (done)
52	  - Port `build` (keep shrink / compress / finalize on the

59	    `BD_*` config.
60	  - Validate: `capture-test` + `write-test` self-tests green.
61	- 0.3.0-4 docs: README + ARCHITECTURE + deferred todo (done)
62	  - README per-subcommand usage (incl. the `--add` / `@file`
63	    syntax + collision rules + rootless note), ARCHITECTURE
64	    module map.
65	  - Add the installable-packages todo (tier-two).
66	- 0.3.0-5 chore: add justfile (done)

79	    time.

81	## Todo
82	
83	 Entries are in **strict priority rank** — #1 highest,
84	 descending. Reprioritize by moving an entry, then
85	 `vc-x1 fix-todo --no-dry-run notes/todo.md` to renumber.
86	 The numbers are positional rank, not stable IDs — to refer
87	 to a Todo, name it by its **title** (a greppable mention;
88	 a numbered list item has no anchor to link to), not its
89	 number. Long-tail entries
90	 live in [todo-backlog.md](todo-backlog.md). Use the
91	 [Prose Form in AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md#prose-form); deeper
92	 detail goes in `notes/chores/chores-NN.md` design
93	 subsections (link via `[N]` ref).
94	
95	1. **Handle installable packages in build inject.** Today `bd-build
96	   --add` only file-copies into the rootfs (the rootless `debugfs`
97	   path). A second tier would recognize an installable package
98	   (`.deb` / Alpine `apk` / Python wheel) and install it *properly*
99	   (dependencies, post-install hooks) instead of copying files.
100	   - This needs loop-mount + `chroot` + `qemu-user` / `binfmt_misc`
101	     (and root) to run the distro's installer in a foreign-arch
102	     rootfs — out of scope for the rootless KISS path, so it is its
103	     own cycle.
104	   - The `--add` fallback (user supplies the explicit destination)
105	     stays the path for anything not recognized.
106	
107	## Done
108	
109	Completed tasks are moved from `## Todo` to here, `## Done`, as they are completed
110	and older `## Done` sections are moved to [done.md](done.md) to keep this file small.
```

This is the final push of the cycle, so my wrap-up comes before the invoke.

**Cycle 0.3.0 complete — fc's image builder ported into bdimager, generalized.** What landed on `main`:

- **`0.3.0-0`** scaffold: installable uv/hatchling package, `bd-config.toml` version-of-record, `bd-*` console scripts.
- **`0.3.0-1`** rootless storage + config core (`imglib`/`imgcfg`/`devsafe`/`mk_test_image`), de-fc'd.
- **`0.3.0-2`** build app + generic `--add`/`@file` injection (`:`/`->` separators, absolute-dest disambiguation, overwrite).
- **`0.3.0-3`** capture + write device apps (full safety gate + expand), `--test-mode`.
- **`0.3.0-4`** README + ARCHITECTURE + the deferred installable-packages todo.
- **`0.3.0-5`** justfile (your request) — wraps the apps, pytest, and the rootless smokes.

18 pytest tests green; the full capture→build→write→expand chain self-tests rootless; `just image-test`/`capture-test`/`write-test` exercise the installed scripts. Real-device write (sudo `dd` on hardware) remains the one untested path, by nature.

Per the cycle protocol I'll stop after this final push — next steps are yours to direct.
